You are the task coordinator for a UAV search-and-rescue team after a flood. You get the list of open incidents and one new field report. Choose exactly ONE action and reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else.

Actions:
1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

Example 1
Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 09:12:
"Two people waving from a rooftop at Boat Jetty. Clear daylight."
Answer: {"action":"CREATE_VERIFICATION","landmark":"Boat Jetty","sensor":"RGB","priority":2}

Example 2
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | RGB | priority 2
New report from UAV3 at 21:40:
"Smoke has covered Clinic Gate now and the water is rising around the people."
Answer: {"action":"UPDATE_INCIDENT","incident":"I2","landmark":"Clinic Gate","sensor":"THERMAL","priority":3}

Example 3
Open incidents:
- I4 | Canal Lock | ANY | priority 1
New report from Ground team at 11:05:
"False alarm at Canal Lock. It was a tarp, nobody there."
Answer: {"action":"CLOSE_INCIDENT","incident":"I4"}

Example 4
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | THERMAL | priority 3
New report from UAV2 at 10:30:
"Battery at 35 percent, returning to the charging point."
Answer: {"action":"NO_ACTION"}

Open incidents:
- I13 | Rice Field B | THERMAL | priority 2
New report from UAV3 at 13:08:
"Re I13: UAV3: Rice Field B is resolved, all rescued."
Answer with one JSON object.

You are the task coordinator for a UAV search-and-rescue team after a flood. You get the list of open incidents and one new field report. Choose exactly ONE action and reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else.

Actions:
1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

Example 1
Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 09:12:
"Two people waving from a rooftop at Boat Jetty. Clear daylight."
Answer: {"action":"CREATE_VERIFICATION","landmark":"Boat Jetty","sensor":"RGB","priority":2}

Example 2
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | RGB | priority 2
New report from UAV3 at 21:40:
"Smoke has covered Clinic Gate now and the water is rising around the people."
Answer: {"action":"UPDATE_INCIDENT","incident":"I2","landmark":"Clinic Gate","sensor":"THERMAL","priority":3}

- I4 | Canal Lock | ANY | priority 1

"False alarm at Canal Lock. It was a tarp, nobody there."

1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

Example 1
Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 09:12:
"Two people waving from a rooftop at Boat Jetty. Clear daylight."
Answer: {"action":"CREATE_VERIFICATION","landmark":"Boat Jetty","sensor":"RGB","priority":2}

Example 2
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | RGB | priority 2
New report from UAV3 at 21:40:
"Smoke has covered Clinic Gate now and the water is rising around the people."
Answer: {"action":"UPDATE_INCIDENT","incident":"I2","landmark":"Clinic Gate","sensor":"THERMAL","priority":3}

Example 3
Open incidents:
- I4 | Canal Lock | ANY | priority 1
New report from Ground team at 11:05:
"False alarm at Canal Lock. It was a tarp, nobody there."
Answer: {"action":"CLOSE_INCIDENT","incident":"I4"}

Example 4
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | THERMAL | priority 3
New report from UAV2 at 10:30:
"Battery at 35 percent, returning to the charging point."
Answer: {"action":"NO_ACTION"}

Open incidents:
- I4 | Clinic Gate | RGB | priority 1
- I11 | Canal Lock | RGB | priority 3
New report from UAV1 at 19:02:
"UAV1: Fuel Station is empty, nobody there."
Answer with one JSON object.

You are the task coordinator for a UAV search-and-rescue team after a flood. You get the list of open incidents and one new field report. Choose exactly ONE action and reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else.

Actions:
1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

Example 1
Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 09:12:
"Two people waving from a rooftop at Boat Jetty. Clear daylight."
Answer: {"action":"CREATE_VERIFICATION","landmark":"Boat Jetty","sensor":"RGB","priority":2}

Example 2
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | RGB | priority 2
New report from UAV3 at 21:40:
"Smoke has covered Clinic Gate now and the water is rising around the people."
Answer: {"action":"UPDATE_INCIDENT","incident":"I2","landmark":"Clinic Gate","sensor":"THERMAL","priority":3}

Example 3
Open incidents:
- I4 | Canal Lock | ANY | priority 1
New report from Ground team at 11:05:
"False alarm at Canal Lock. It was a tarp, nobody there."
Answer: {"action":"CLOSE_INCIDENT","incident":"I4"}

Example 4
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | THERMAL | priority 3
New report from UAV2 at 10:30:
"Battery at 35 percent, returning to the charging point."
Answer: {"action":"NO_ACTION"}

Open incidents:
- I10 | School Ground | RGB | priority 3
- I14 | Temple Junction | ANY | priority 2
New report from Ground team at 06:05:
"Ground team: Temple Junction looks the same as before. Next waypoint reached."

You are the task coordinator for a UAV search-and-rescue team after a flood. You get the list of open incidents and one new field report. Choose exactly ONE action and reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else.

Actions:
1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

You are the task coordinator for a UAV search-and-rescue team after a flood. You get the list of open incidents and one new field report. Choose exactly ONE action and reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else.

Actions:
1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

Example 1
Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 09:12:
"Two people waving from a rooftop at Boat Jetty. Clear daylight."
Answer: {"action":"CREATE_VERIFICATION","landmark":"Boat Jetty","sensor":"RGB","priority":2}

Example 2
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | RGB | priority 2
New report from UAV3 at 21:40:
"Smoke has covered Clinic Gate now and the water is rising around the people."
Answer: {"action":"UPDATE_INCIDENT","incident":"I2","landmark":"Clinic Gate","sensor":"THERMAL","priority":3}

Example 3
Open incidents:
- I4 | Canal Lock | ANY | priority 1
New report from Ground team at 11:05:
"False alarm at Canal Lock. It was a tarp, nobody there."
Answer: {"action":"CLOSE_INCIDENT","incident":"I4"}

Example 4
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | THERMAL | priority 3
New report from UAV2 at 10:30:
"Battery at 35 percent, returning to the charging point."
Answer: {"action":"NO_ACTION"}

Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 14:49:
"UAV1: School Ground is empty, nobody there."
Answer with one JSON object.

2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

Example 1
Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 09:12:
"Two people waving from a rooftop at Boat Jetty. Clear daylight."
Answer: {"action":"CREATE_VERIFICATION","landmark":"Boat Jetty","sensor":"RGB","priority":2}

Answer with one JSON object.

You are the task coordinator for a UAV search-and-rescue team after a flood. You get the list of open incidents and one new field report. Choose exactly ONE action and reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else.

Actions:
1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

- I5 | Hill Road Bend | ANY | priority 2
New report from UAV2 at 22:41:
"UAV2: at Hill Road Bend now. Thick fog. The visible camera cannot see anything there."
Answer with one JSON object.

You are the task coordinator for a UAV search-and-rescue team after a flood. You get the list of open incidents and one new field report. Choose exactly ONE action and reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else.

Actions:
1. CREATE_VERIFICATION - the report describes people who may need help at a place that has NO open incident. Give landmark, sensor, priority.
2. UPDATE_INCIDENT - the report changes an open incident: the danger is worse, a different sensor is needed, or the people moved. Give the incident id and the full updated record (landmark, sensor, priority).
3. CLOSE_INCIDENT - the report says an open incident is resolved or was a false alarm. Give the incident id.
4. NO_ACTION - routine status, no people found, or nothing new about an open incident.

Sensor: THERMAL if it is dark, or there is smoke or fog. RGB if the report says visibility is clear in daylight. ANY if the report does not say.
Priority: 3 = child, elderly or injured person, or immediate danger (rising water, fire, collapse). 2 = people confirmed, no immediate danger. 1 = possible sighting, not confirmed.
Landmarks: Temple Junction, North Bridge, Rice Field A, Rice Field B, School Ground, Clinic Gate, Boat Jetty, Railway Crossing, Market Square, Fuel Station, Hill Road Bend, Canal Lock.

Example 1
Open incidents: none
New report from UAV1 at 09:12:
"Two people waving from a rooftop at Boat Jetty. Clear daylight."
Answer: {"action":"CREATE_VERIFICATION","landmark":"Boat Jetty","sensor":"RGB","priority":2}

Example 2
Open incidents:
- I2 | Clinic Gate | RGB | priority 2
New report from UAV3 at 21:40:
"Smoke has covered Clinic Gate now and the water is rising around the people."
Answer: {"action":"UPDATE_INCIDENT","incident":"I2","landmark":"Clinic Gate","sensor":"THERMAL","priority":3}

Open incidents:
- I4 | Canal Lock | ANY | priority 1
New report from Ground team at 11:05:
"False alarm at Canal Lock. It was a tarp, nobody there."
